Citation Catalog v2 — profile-first

A separate review page: it is not linked from, and does not feed, the packet or the candidate list. Generated 2026-11-10 from 443 held citations that have a PDF on file.
How it reads a paper. 1) Work out how the paper cites: its bibliography format and which in-text style it actually uses ([n], (n), superscript, author-year). 2) Search for your publication's marker only in that style — in a "[3] Smith…" paper a "(3)" is a list item and is never read as a citation. 3) Quote the whole sentence with page and section; text in a table is tied to its caption ("Listed in Table N"). 4) Compare with what the database holds. Page headers and page numbers are removed before anything is counted.
